Let x = n * n.
Let a = shift_sint32(board_0, 0).
Let a_1 = memcpy(Mint_0, a, Mint_undef_0, a, x).
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(i) /\ is_sint32(n).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(board_0.base) <= 0) /\ linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_valid_board(Malloc_0, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: 0 <= x.
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: (0 <= i) /\ (i <= x).
  (* Invariant 'i_2' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < i) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, i_1)] = 0))).
  (* Else *)

}

      let a_4 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_1, i)) in ((0 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      (a_3[a_4] = Mint_0[a_4]))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_41' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) != x_1))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_47' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) < x_1))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_57' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> ((i + (n * i_1)) < (j + x)))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n <= j.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < j) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(j, row_0, i))] = 0))).
}
Prove: P_rows_empty_from(a_1, j, board_0, row_0).

      ((i_2 < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_3, i_2) != x_5))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_47' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_3, i_2) < x_5))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_57' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> ((i_2 + (n * i_3)) < x_3))))).
  (* Then *)
  Have: j < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)
  Have: (-1) <= j.
  (* Invariant 'i_22' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_2, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_23' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_2, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_2, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_25' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_2, n, board_0, x_4).
  (* Invariant 'i_26' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 <= j) ->
      (a_2[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i_2))] = 0))).

      let a_3 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_3, i_2)) in ((0 <= i_3) ->

      (a_2[a_3] = Mint_0[a_3]))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_41' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> (x_1 != L_idx(n, i_3, i_2)))))).

  (* Goal *)

  (* Pre-condition *)

  (* Pre-condition *)

  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, x_4).
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)

  (* Invariant 'i_43' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, row_0, i) < x_5))).
  (* Invariant 'i_44' *)
  Have: forall i_2,i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_1) ->

  (* Invariant 'i_52' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < row_0) -> ((n + (n * i)) <= x_1))).
  (* Invariant 'i_53' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> ((i + (n * i_1)) < x_1))))).

  Have: (0 <= j) /\ (j <= n).

  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).

  (* Invariant 'i_38' *)
  Have: x_2 < x_4.

  Else { Have: a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, x_2) <- 0] = Mint_0. }
  (* Invariant 'i_9' *)
  Have: (-1) <= j.

  Have: P_rows_solved(a_3, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)

  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)

  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z.
      let a_4 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_1, i)) in ((0 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      (a_3[a_4] = Mint_0[a_4]))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < j) ->
      (a_3[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i))] =
       (if (i = col_0) then 1 else 0)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((j <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      (a_3[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i))] = 0))).
  (* Invariant 'i_39' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. let x_5 = n * i in ((0 <= i) -> ((i < row_0) ->
      ((x_1 < (n + x_5)) -> ((x_5 <= x_1) -> false)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_45' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) != L_idx(n, row_0, j)))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n <= j.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(j, row_0, col_0))] = 1.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((i != col_0) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < j) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(j, row_0, i))] = 0)))).

Goal Assertion (file /results/test-03-01/deepseek-flash/test-03-01-28.cbs/.col2inv-trustc-ah4ah1mz.c, line 374):
Let x = n * row_0.
Let a = shift_sint32(board_0, x).
Let x_1 = n * n.
Let a_1 = memcpy(Mint_0, a, Mint_undef_0, a, x_1 - x).
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(col_0) /\ is_sint32(n) /\ is_sint32(row_0).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(board_0.base) <= 0) /\ linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_valid_board(Malloc_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: (row_0 <= n) /\ (0 <= row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_binary_board(Mint_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Else *)
  Have: row_0 != n.
  (* Invariant 'i_29' *)
  Have: row_0 < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: 0 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: (0 <= col_0) /\ (col_0 <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_31' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_32' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_33' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_34' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Then *)
  Have: col_0 < n.
  (* Call 'is_safe' *)
  Have: P_safe_position(a_1, n, board_0, row_0, col_0).
}
Prove: (n + x) <= x_1.
Prover CVC5 1.1.2 returns Valid (Qed:15ms) (3.3s) (338544)

Goal Instance of 'Pre-condition (file /results/test-03-01/deepseek-flash/test-03-01-28.cbs/.col2inv-trustc-ah4ah1mz.c, line 258) in 'clear_row'' in 'solve_from_row' at call 'clear_row' (file /results/test-03-01/deepseek-flash/test-03-01-28.cbs/.col2inv-trustc-ah4ah1mz.c, line 380)
:
Let x = n * row_0.
Let a = shift_sint32(board_0, x).
Let x_1 = n * n.
Let a_1 = memcpy(Mint_0, a, Mint_undef_0, a, x_1 - x).
Let a_2 = memcpy(a_1, a, Mint_undef_1, a, n).
Let x_2 = 1 + row_0.
Let x_3 = n * x_2.
Let a_3 = shift_sint32(board_0, x_3).
Let a_4 = memcpy(a_2, a_3, Mint_undef_2, a_3, x_1 - x_3).
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(col_0) /\ is_sint32(n) /\ is_sint32(row_0).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(board_0.base) <= 0) /\ linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_valid_board(Malloc_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: (row_0 <= n) /\ (0 <= row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_binary_board(Mint_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Else *)
  Have: row_0 != n.
  (* Invariant 'i_29' *)
  Have: row_0 < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: 0 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: (0 <= col_0) /\ (col_0 <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_31' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_32' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_33' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_34' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Then *)
  Have: col_0 < n.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: (n + x) <= x_1.
  (* Call 'place_queen' *)
  Have: P_safe_position(a_1, n, board_0, row_0, col_0) /\
      P_binary_board(a_2, n, board_0) /\
      P_rows_solved(a_2, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_rows_empty_from(a_2, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_no_attack_prefix(a_2, n, board_0, x_2).
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: x_3 <= x_1.
  (* Call 'solve_from_row' *)
  Have: ((-1) <= row_0) /\ P_binary_board(a_4, n, board_0) /\
      P_rows_solved(a_4, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_rows_empty_from(a_4, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_no_attack_prefix(a_4, n, board_0, x_2).
}
Prove: P_rows_solved(a_4, n, board_0, row_0).
